- **traps**: Agent sessions on this repo find these environment traps many times. They are facts for contributors, not user docs. Each entry gives the symptom, then the fix. Remove an entry when it is no longer true. ([Stevie1704/sil](https://github.com/Stevie1704/sil/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/traps.md))
- **traps-async**: Read before writing code that awaits, forks, subscribes, or depends on the order of Messages or frames. ([doeixd/foldkit-plus](https://github.com/doeixd/foldkit-plus/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/traps-async.md) · ★41)
- **traps-bundling-and-builds**: Read before changing a package's build (tsdown), exports or declarations, a bundle budget, or adding an import that may pull a namespace or a server into a page. ([doeixd/foldkit-plus](https://github.com/doeixd/foldkit-plus/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/traps-bundling-and-builds.md) · ★41)
- **traps-claims**: Read before claiming something is absent, done or retried, or before editing a README, the changelog, a design note or a commit message. ([doeixd/foldkit-plus](https://github.com/doeixd/foldkit-plus/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/traps-claims.md) · ★41)
- **traps-cloudflare-and-servers**: Read before touching a Worker, the Miniflare or Wrangler stack, D1, auth, an RPC or WebSocket server, or server logging. ([doeixd/foldkit-plus](https://github.com/doeixd/foldkit-plus/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/traps-cloudflare-and-servers.md) · ★41)
- **traps-css-and-layout**: Read before styling or laying out a component, a theme knob, an animation or an overlay. ([doeixd/foldkit-plus](https://github.com/doeixd/foldkit-plus/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/traps-css-and-layout.md) · ★41)
- **traps-data-and-sql**: Read before touching Remote, Entity, Guard, Sync, Form, a live read, or the Drizzle, SQLite or Postgres code. ([doeixd/foldkit-plus](https://github.com/doeixd/foldkit-plus/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/traps-data-and-sql.md) · ★41)
- **traps-effect-4**: Read before writing Effect code from memory, bumping Effect, or using a renamed or removed API. Check the installed `.d.ts` before reaching for a remembered API. ([doeixd/foldkit-plus](https://github.com/doeixd/foldkit-plus/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/traps-effect-4.md) · ★41)
- **traps-external-apis**: Read before writing or testing an adapter for a library or protocol we do not own (Agent Native, WebMCP, a wire client), or a schema boundary an agent calls. ([doeixd/foldkit-plus](https://github.com/doeixd/foldkit-plus/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/traps-external-apis.md) · ★41)
- **traps-foldkit-runtime**: Read before touching a Mount, Subscription, Command, lazy slot, embed or runtime boot, a URL or route Message, a Scene test, or an upstream component bundle (Menu, Listbox, Button, Dialog). ([doeixd/foldkit-plus](https://github.com/doeixd/foldkit-plus/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/traps-foldkit-runtime.md) · ★41)
- **traps-ssr-and-rendering**: Read before touching server rendering, hydration, Resume or the loader entry, head tags in the template, or a page cache. ([doeixd/foldkit-plus](https://github.com/doeixd/foldkit-plus/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/traps-ssr-and-rendering.md) · ★41)
- **traps-tests-browser**: Read before writing or debugging a `*.browser.test.ts`, an e2e test, a touch or pointer test, or a layout assertion. ([doeixd/foldkit-plus](https://github.com/doeixd/foldkit-plus/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/traps-tests-browser.md) · ★41)
- **traps-tests-general**: Read before writing or reviewing any test, fixture, seeded walk or race test. ([doeixd/foldkit-plus](https://github.com/doeixd/foldkit-plus/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/traps-tests-general.md) · ★41)
- **traps-tests-mutation**: Read before writing a mutation spec, running pnpm mutate, or judging a surviving mutation. Tooling limits of `pnpm mutate` (what it cannot reach) are in `traps-tooling-vitest-mutate.md`. ([doeixd/foldkit-plus](https://github.com/doeixd/foldkit-plus/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/traps-tests-mutation.md) · ★41)
- **traps-tooling-ci-npm**: Read before changing a dependency or lockfile, a publish or release step, a CI job, or running a Jev review. ([doeixd/foldkit-plus](https://github.com/doeixd/foldkit-plus/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/traps-tooling-ci-npm.md) · ★41)
- **traps-tooling-git**: Read before staging, committing, moving files, rebuilding a commit through a temporary index, or restarting a branch. The rules these come from are in `AGENTS.md` ("Git in the shared tree"). ([doeixd/foldkit-plus](https://github.com/doeixd/foldkit-plus/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/traps-tooling-git.md) · ★41)
- **traps-tooling-shell**: Read before writing a file through a script, chaining commands with && or a pipe, or formatting. ([doeixd/foldkit-plus](https://github.com/doeixd/foldkit-plus/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/traps-tooling-shell.md) · ★41)
- **traps-tooling-typescript-packaging**: Read before changing a tsconfig, a package export, how a workspace package resolves, a Vite config, or running tsc. ([doeixd/foldkit-plus](https://github.com/doeixd/foldkit-plus/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/traps-tooling-typescript-packaging.md) · ★41)
- **traps-tooling-vitest-mutate**: Read before running Vitest or pnpm mutate, adding a Vitest config or alias, or driving a dev server and browser. ([doeixd/foldkit-plus](https://github.com/doeixd/foldkit-plus/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/traps-tooling-vitest-mutate.md) · ★41)
- **traps-types**: Read before writing a public type, a generic signature, a type test, or an Option-returning API. ([doeixd/foldkit-plus](https://github.com/doeixd/foldkit-plus/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/traps-types.md) · ★41)
